PDF WORKBENCH PWA — MILESTONE 3.4.4 CURRENT UPDATE
=====================================================

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.4.4
Visible About version: 3.4.4
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m3.4.4-v1

WHY THIS REVISION EXISTS
------------------------
Device testing of 3.4.3 indicates that page/template source selection and insertion position now function correctly in Pages, single View, and split View across Continuous, Page Snap, and Full Page. A subtler same-document split case remained: when the same PDF was open in both panes, inserting a page in one pane before the logical page visible in the other pane changed the other pane's apparent scroll position. Inserting after/below the other pane did not move it.

ROOT CAUSE
----------
The shared document page list changed correctly, and the inactive pane retained the same active page id. However, the inactive pane also retained a raw scrollTop measured against the old page stack. Inserting a page above that pane moves all later page stages downward, so restoring the old pixel scrollTop no longer shows the same logical content.

3.4.4 FIX
---------
- Before insertion, if the other split pane shows the same document, capture that pane's logical page and the document point under the pane center.
- Invalidate the inactive pane's stale raw scroll coordinates for that structural edit.
- After rebuilding the pane, restore the same logical page/content point under the pane center.
- In Page Snap, keep the same logical page centered.
- In Full Page, preserve the same active page id; no continuous-scroll offset repair is required.
- The pane receiving the insertion still focuses the inserted page exactly as in 3.4.3.
- This change is intentionally limited to the inactive same-document split pane; the now-working Pages and single-View insertion/template paths are not redesigned.

ARCHITECTURAL RULE
------------------
THE DOCUMENT IS SHARED; THE VIEW IS NOT. A structural document edit may change page indices, but an independent viewer instance should remain anchored to the same logical page/content whenever that page still exists.

IMMEDIATE 3.4.4 TESTS
---------------------
1. Same PDF in both panes, right pane later in the document. Insert on the left before the right pane's page; right should not move.
2. Insert on the left after/below the right pane's page; right should also not move.
3. Repeat in Continuous, Page Snap, and Full Page.
4. Reverse left/right roles.
5. Regression-check template source/insertion in Pages, single View, and split View.

RELEASE PRACTICE
----------------
Every distribution ZIP must include this handoff, README, and graph-paper-reference.png and must keep the visible About version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.


PDF WORKBENCH PWA — MILESTONE 3.4.3 CURRENT UPDATE
=====================================================

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.4.3
Visible About version: 3.4.3
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m3.4.3-v1

WHY THIS REVISION EXISTS
------------------------
3.4.2 fixed the recent ordinary single-View state regressions. Device testing then showed that Pages and single View copied/saved templates and inserted pages correctly, but split View remained sporadic: it could save the wrong (often neighboring) page as a template, and repeated insertions could land before, after, or several pages away from the visibly current page.

IMPORTANT DIAGNOSIS
-------------------
The underlying page/template/insertion model is sound because Pages and single View work correctly. The remaining defect was split-pane page identity. Split mode was still using the IntersectionObserver's most-visible entry within an individual callback to update view.activePageId. That callback does not represent all currently visible pages and can fire in timing-dependent groups, so a neighboring page can become authoritative even when another page is visually centered.

3.4.3 FIX
---------
- Added an independent viewport-center current-page calculation for each split pane.
- Each pane's page identity now comes from its own scrollTop/clientHeight and actual page-stage geometry.
- Split scroll events schedule this synchronization on requestAnimationFrame.
- Activating a pane synchronizes its visible page before toolbar operations use it.
- Opening the Insert Page menu in split mode synchronizes the active pane and then captures documentId/pageId/paneId as the immutable command target.
- Save current page as template in split mode uses that same captured target.
- IntersectionObserver in split mode now controls only render/release of canvases, not current-page identity.
- Programmatic pane rebuild/restoration synchronizes the visible page after layout settles.
- Existing pending-page-focus protection remains in place during insertion, so a rebuilt pane cannot choose a different page before the inserted page is focused.

DO NOT REGRESS
--------------
- The previous Milestone 2 split-view requirement remains: left/right are independent VIEW INSTANCES, including when the same PDF is displayed in both panes.
- The older split/single scroll work concerned independent pane state. The ordinary single-document switching regression seen during 3.4.1 testing was new and was fixed in 3.4.2.
- Pages-based template saving/insertion and single-View template saving/insertion were reported working in 3.4.2 and should not be redesigned as part of this fix.
- Preserve finger pan/pinch, pen-reserved-for-ink behavior, Presentation tap/hover behavior, export/manipulation functions, JBIG2/WASM configuration, and session templates.

IMMEDIATE 3.4.3 TESTS
---------------------
1. Split + Page Snap: left and right on clearly different pages; save a template from each and verify exact source page.
2. Insert 5-6 pages successively from one split pane, mixing blank, graph, duplicate, and saved template pages. Each should go directly after the visible/new current page.
3. Repeat in the other pane.
4. Same PDF in both panes at different page positions; template/insert must use only the active pane's page.
5. Different PDFs in the two panes; repeat.
6. Regression check Pages and single View.

RELEASE PRACTICE
----------------
Every distribution ZIP must include this handoff, README, and graph-paper-reference.png and must keep the visible About version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.


PDF WORKBENCH PWA — MILESTONE 3.4.2 CURRENT UPDATE
=====================================================

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.4.2
Visible About version: 3.4.2
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m3.4.2-v1

THIS SECTION IS AUTHORITATIVE OVER OLDER STATUS TEXT BELOW.

RECENT 3.4.1 TEST FINDINGS
--------------------------
The 3.4.1 insertion patch exposed/confirmed a set of ordinary single-View synchronization problems:
- switching between ordinary open documents could apply the previous document's scroll location to the newly selected document;
- double-clicking a thumbnail in Pages could enter View at the top instead of at the selected page;
- in Page Snap mode, Save current page as template could capture a neighboring page (apparently often the next page);
- the first View insertion could land correctly but subsequent insertions could use the wrong side of the visibly current page.

Important diagnostic result: the same template save/insert operations work correctly when initiated from Pages. Therefore the document page model and template/page-copy machinery are considered sound; the fault is in single-View current-page/scroll synchronization.

Do NOT conflate the ordinary document-switch issue with the older Milestone 2 split-view work. The earlier split work concerned preserving independent left/right pane state, including same-document and different-document panes. The ordinary single-view switch behavior reported here is a newly discovered/current regression.

ROOT CAUSES / 3.4.2 FIX
------------------------
1. The single viewer previously let IntersectionObserver callback timing decide which page became current. In Page Snap/Continuous View, observer callback entry batches can promote a neighboring page even when another page is visibly centered.
2. When switching documents, loadDocumentState saved the outgoing document correctly, loaded the incoming document state, and then called renderAll() while the OLD document was still in the viewer DOM. renderAll() immediately saved that stale DOM scroll position into the newly selected document.
3. Pages double-click set activePageId but left a numeric saved scroll offset in the view. The subsequent render could restore that offset (often the top) instead of centering the requested page.

3.4.2 changes:
- in ordinary Continuous/Page Snap View, the page nearest the actual viewport center is authoritative;
- single-view scrolling schedules a lightweight viewport-center page synchronization;
- opening Insert in ordinary View explicitly synchronizes the visible page before capturing the page/template target;
- saving ordinary single-view state synchronizes the visible page before persisting page identity;
- document switching renders the incoming document with renderAll({saveState:false}), preventing the outgoing DOM from overwriting the incoming document's saved state;
- pending single-view page-sync animation frames are cancelled during document changes/rebuilds;
- Pages double-click clears stale single-view scroll restoration, focus-locks the chosen page, rebuilds View, and explicitly centers/renders that page;
- the 3.4.1 exact insertion-target and post-insert focus protection remains in place;
- no intended changes to split-pane state architecture, template session lifetime, narrow-toolbar behavior, touch/pen input, Presentation, output operations, or JBIG2/WASM configuration.

IMMEDIATE 3.4.2 TESTS
---------------------
1. Page Snap: visibly center page N; open Insert and verify Save page N as template is shown and saves the correct page.
2. Repeated View insertion: insert several blank/graph/template pages in succession; each should follow the page visibly current when Insert was opened and the new page should become current.
3. Pages: double-click several thumbnails far from page 1 and verify View opens at exactly those pages.
4. Open two documents, place them at very different scroll positions, and switch back/forth repeatedly; each must restore its own location.
5. Reconfirm Pages-based template save/insert.
6. Regression-test split view (same/different documents), Presentation, iPad/Surface/Chromebook finger/pen behavior, Files multi-export ZIP, Combine/Split, and BaakeScan/JBIG2.

DISTRIBUTION RULE
-----------------
Every release ZIP must contain this comprehensive handoff, README, and graph-paper reference and keep visible About version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.


PDF WORKBENCH PWA — MILESTONE 3.4.1 CURRENT UPDATE
=====================================================

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.4.1
Visible About version: 3.4.1
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m3.4.1-v1

THIS SECTION IS AUTHORITATIVE OVER OLDER STATUS TEXT BELOW.

RECENT TEST FINDING
-------------------
Milestone 3.4.0 templates worked as expected, including remaining available after Close all files during the same session. However, page insertion had a repeat-insertion bug: the first inserted page was generally correct, but subsequent inserts could appear several pages later or at the end of the document. View could also stay on “Rendering…” after insertion even though the page appeared correctly in Pages and rendered after switching away and back.

ROOT CAUSE / 3.4.1 FIX
----------------------
The insertion path cleared the saved scroll position so the newly inserted page could become current, but then immediately saved the OLD pre-insertion View DOM scroll position back into the view state before rebuilding the viewer. Because the page list had changed, that stale scroll offset could correspond to a different page. IntersectionObserver could then promote that different page to current before the intended inserted page was focused, causing later Insert commands to use the wrong anchor and sometimes leaving the intended page unrendered until View was rebuilt.

3.4.1 changes:
- opening Insert captures the exact document/page/pane insertion target;
- the chosen command uses that captured target rather than recomputing current page after menu/layout activity;
- structural insertion saves document state without reading stale pre-edit viewer scroll offsets;
- the inserted page is focus-locked while the rebuilt viewer settles so observer callbacks cannot replace it prematurely;
- the inserted page is explicitly scrolled into view and its render is started after layout;
- Save page as template uses the same captured-page semantics;
- no changes to template session lifetime, narrow toolbar rules, touch/pen input, Files/output operations, split-state architecture, or JBIG2/WASM configuration.

IMMEDIATE 3.4.1 TESTS
---------------------
1. Insert several pages successively in the middle of a document in Continuous View; each should follow the page that was current when Insert was opened.
2. Verify every inserted page becomes current and does not remain at Rendering….
3. Repeat in Page Snap and Full Page.
4. Repeat from Pages using a selected page.
5. Repeat in Presentation and Split View, including same-document split panes.
6. Confirm session templates still save/insert/rename/delete and survive Close all files.
7. Regression-test touch/pen, narrow Surface toolbar, Files export/ZIP, Combine/Split, and BaakeScan/JBIG2.

DISTRIBUTION RULE
-----------------
Every release ZIP must contain this comprehensive handoff, README, and graph-paper reference and keep visible About version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.

RECENT VALIDATION / BASELINE
----------------------------
The user reported Milestone 3.3.0 functionality working smoothly on all three tested device classes: Surface, iPad, and Chromebook. The main responsive issue observed is the narrow Surface window, where the active-document selector and other top controls compete with View / Pages / Files.

MILESTONE 3.4.0 — SESSION PAGE TEMPLATES
-----------------------------------------
Reusable page templates are now implemented as SESSION templates. They intentionally do not use persistent IndexedDB/OPFS storage yet; persistence should be added together with the later full Files/Library architecture to avoid building a temporary storage model that must later be migrated.

Template workflow:
- The shared Insert Page menu in Pages, regular View, and Presentation contains a Session templates section.
- Choose Save current/selected page as template.
- In regular View/Presentation, save the current page.
- In Pages, save the selection-anchor/most recently selected page when available.
- The naming prompt is prefilled with the first available generic name Template 1, Template 2, etc. This is deliberate so a temporary template can be saved without spending time naming it.
- Saved templates immediately appear in the Insert Page menu and can be inserted repeatedly as fresh independent page instances.
- Manage templates allows rename and delete.

Captured template geometry/content:
- preserve source/background page content;
- preserve page size;
- preserve current page rotation/orientation;
- generated blank/graph pages can also be templates;
- future annotation support should add an explicit save-template with/without annotations choice.

Session lifetime:
- templates remain available during the current run even after Close all files;
- source PDF/image data needed by a session template is retained in memory while any template references it;
- deleting the final template/document reference allows that source to be released;
- app reload/restart intentionally clears templates in 3.4.0;
- persistent templates belong in the later Files/Library storage milestone and should participate in Library backup/export.

NARROW-WINDOW TOOLBAR POLICY
----------------------------
Responsive priority based on the supplied Surface screenshots:
1. Preserve View / Pages / Files as primary workspace navigation.
2. Shrink/truncate the active-document filename selector first.
3. Secondary viewer controls may remain icon-only at narrow widths.
4. Only consider collapsing workspace navigation at extremely narrow sizes if later testing proves it necessary.

3.4.0 changes the toolbar flex rules so View / Pages / Files have stable minimum widths and the active-document selector can shrink to a compact ellipsized selector in narrow windows. Do not return to progressive squeezing of the workspace buttons.

FILES / OUTPUT BASELINE TO PRESERVE
-----------------------------------
Files uses one shared checkbox list of open documents and collapsible New / Export / Extract / Split / Combine sections.
- one checked document exports as PDF;
- multiple checked documents export as separate PDFs inside one ZIP;
- Combine uses checked documents but its own independent reorder list;
- Split uses the active document and packages multiple outputs in one ZIP;
- Extract uses selected Pages from the active document;
- new blank/graph documents default to US Letter landscape.

PAGE INSERTION BASELINE TO PRESERVE
-----------------------------------
Insert Page remains available in Pages, regular View, and Presentation with:
- duplicate with annotations;
- duplicate without annotations;
- blank matching current displayed size/orientation;
- graph paper matching current displayed size/orientation;
- saved Session templates.

Graph paper is generated, not rasterized: light cyan vector lines, 18-point (1/4-inch) spacing, 9-point margin, white background, based on graph-paper-reference.png.

OTHER ROADMAP ITEMS RECENTLY AGREED
-----------------------------------
- Copy/move selected pages from one open document to another without merging whole documents; preserve page geometry/rotation and eventually allow annotation inclusion choice.
- Full Files/Library persistence: internally retained documents/projects, Close/reopen, Recent, folders, rename/search/sort, Remove from Recent, Delete Stored Copy, backup/export, and persistent templates.
- Split on iPad: keep multiple split results in one ZIP rather than opening many PDF previews.
- Future inking should be available in regular View and Presentation with shared tool state; pen inks, one finger pans, two fingers pinch/zoom, visible UI remains pen-clickable, Draw with Finger default Off.
- Once ink exists, test Full Page bottom navigation arrows for palm interference and consider auto-hide/palm-safe/optional hiding.

REGRESSION-SENSITIVE BASELINE
-----------------------------
Do not casually alter:
- one-finger pan/scroll and two-finger pinch/zoom;
- pen does not pan/zoom document content;
- pen operates visible UI controls;
- Pages organizer pen/touch dragging;
- hidden Presentation controls ignore pen hover/tap;
- high-zoom horizontal pan reaches both page edges;
- split/single independent pane state restoration;
- same-document split panes retain independent view state;
- iPad app-level Presentation instead of native fullscreen;
- JBIG2/WASM/cMap/standard-font PDF.js resource configuration;
- structural PDF export engine;
- Split multi-output ZIP behavior and typed group filenames;
- Pages blank-space deselection and direct Extract selected action.

MILESTONE 3.3.0 — FILES WORKSPACE REORGANIZATION
-------------------------------------------------
Files is now the document-level workspace and is available even when no document is open.

The workspace has one always-visible Open documents list with shared checkboxes, followed by expandable/collapsible operation sections:
- New
- Export
- Extract
- Split
- Combine

Shared selection rule:
- Export uses the checked open documents.
- Combine uses the same checked documents, but presents them again in a separate combine-order list with up/down controls. Reordering the Combine list DOES NOT change the general Open documents list.
- Extract is a page-level operation and uses selected pages from the ACTIVE document.
- Split is a single-document operation and uses the ACTIVE document.

The Open documents list marks the active document and allows another document to be made active independently of checkbox selection. It also has Select all and Clear selection actions.

MULTI-DOCUMENT EXPORT
---------------------
Export behavior in Files:
- zero checked documents: export disabled;
- one checked document: export one PDF using the normal structural PDF writer;
- two or more checked documents: export each checked document as its own PDF and package all resulting PDFs into one ZIP.

This ZIP approach is specifically appropriate for iPad, where one PDF naturally enters the iPad preview/share/save flow but a sequence of separate preview windows would be undesirable.

Each PDF in a multi-export uses the document's current working page arrangement. ZIP entry names are based on each document name plus `-edited.pdf`; duplicate names are made unique. ZIP uses STORE compression because PDFs are already compressed and recompressing wastes time/memory.

NEW DOCUMENT LOCATION / DEFAULT ORIENTATION
--------------------------------------------
The permanent top-bar New button has been removed. New document creation now lives under the expandable New section in Files.

New generated documents now default to US Letter LANDSCAPE:
- width 792 PDF points;
- height 612 PDF points.

This affects only brand-new blank/graph-paper documents. Blank/graph pages inserted into existing documents continue to match the current page's displayed size and orientation.

PRESENTATION INSERT CONTROL
---------------------------
The Presentation Insert Page button was formerly a `+` immediately beside the zoom `+`, which was visually ambiguous. It now uses a page-shaped `▱` icon, is placed before the zoom cluster, and has added spacing from the zoom controls. The underlying shared Insert menu and insertion behavior are unchanged.

FILES UI DESIGN PRINCIPLE
-------------------------
Do not make Files one long crowded form. Keep the document list visible and document-level operations compact/collapsible. Reuse shared document selection rather than duplicating a different set of checkboxes inside every operation. Operations may still have their own operation-specific ordering/options (Combine is the key example).

3.2 PAGE CREATION / INSERTION TO PRESERVE
-----------------------------------------
Insert Page remains available from Pages, regular View, and Presentation with the shared choices:
1. Duplicate with annotations.
2. Duplicate without annotations.
3. Blank page matching current displayed size/orientation.
4. Graph-paper page matching current displayed size/orientation.

Until the annotation subsystem exists, the two duplicate choices produce the same visible page but remain distinct commands for future annotation cloning semantics.

Graph paper remains generated, not rasterized: light cyan/blue vector lines, 18-point (1/4-inch) square spacing, 9-point margin, white background. `graph-paper-reference.png` remains bundled.

REGRESSION-SENSITIVE BASELINE
-----------------------------
Do not casually alter:
- one-finger pan/scroll and two-finger pinch/zoom;
- pen does not pan/zoom document content;
- pen operates visible UI controls;
- Pages organizer pen/touch dragging;
- hidden Presentation controls ignore pen hover/tap;
- high-zoom horizontal pan reaches both page edges;
- split/single independent pane state restoration;
- same-document split panes retain independent view state;
- iPad app-level Presentation instead of native fullscreen;
- JBIG2/WASM/cMap/standard-font PDF.js resource configuration;
- structural PDF export engine;
- Split multi-output ZIP behavior and typed group filenames;
- Pages blank-space deselection and direct Extract selected action.

FUTURE FILE/LIBRARY ROADMAP
---------------------------
Open documents are currently runtime working documents. A later Library/persistence milestone should add internal stored copies/projects, Close/reopen, Recent, folders, rename/search/sort, Remove from Recent, Delete Stored Copy, and explicit project persistence. Long-term top-level structure remains Files | Pages | View, with Presentation as a low-chrome form of View.

FUTURE INK UI DECISIONS
-----------------------
Inking should work in regular View and Presentation using the same annotation engine and tool state. Default input model: pen/Pencil inks; one finger pans; two fingers pinch/zoom; pen can operate visible UI; Draw with Finger should default Off. When ink exists, test Full Page bottom navigation controls for accidental palm activation and consider auto-hide/palm-safe/optional hiding if necessary.

NEXT WORK AFTER 3.3 TESTING
---------------------------
Continue Milestone 3 geometry/output features unless testing identifies a regression:
1. page-size normalization and orientation controls;
2. fit/center/crop and margins/expanded canvas;
3. image-to-PDF assembly improvements;
4. compression/target maximum size;
then Library/persistence and later ink/annotations.

DISTRIBUTION RULE
-----------------
Every release ZIP must contain this comprehensive handoff, README, and graph-paper reference and keep visible About version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.

MILESTONE 3.1.2 VALIDATION

MILESTONE 3.2.0 — PAGE / DOCUMENT CREATION
-------------------------------------------
A top-level New button now works even with no external files open:
- Blank document: one US Letter portrait blank page.
- Graph-paper document: one US Letter portrait graph-paper page.

A shared Insert Page After Current menu is available from:
- Pages toolbar: Insert.
- regular View toolbar: + Page.
- Presentation temporary toolbar: compact + icon.

Insert choices:
1. Duplicate with annotations.
2. Duplicate without annotations.
3. Blank page matching current page displayed size/orientation.
4. Graph-paper page matching current page displayed size/orientation.

Annotations do not exist yet, so the two duplicate choices currently produce the same visible result. They are separate commands now so they can diverge when the annotation object model is implemented.

Insertion targeting:
- View/Presentation: current page.
- Split View/Presentation: current page of the ACTIVE pane's document.
- Pages: most recently selected page if it remains selected; otherwise current View page.
- Newly inserted page becomes current. In Pages it also becomes the sole selected page.
- Document edits are shared, so if the same document is visible in both panes the inserted page belongs to both views, while each pane's view position/state remains independent.

GENERATED PAGE MODEL / EXPORT
-----------------------------
Blank and graph pages are generated page instances and do not require a source PDF/image. They participate in organizer editing, Combine, Extract, Split, and Export.

Graph-paper specification captured from the user's supplied reference:
- white background;
- very light cyan/blue square grid;
- thin unobtrusive lines;
- approximately 1/4-inch spacing (implemented as 18 PDF points);
- small clean margin (implemented as 9 points);
- exported graph lines are vector PDF lines, not a raster image.

`graph-paper-reference.png` remains bundled as the original visual reference.

- one finger: pan/scroll;
- two fingers: pinch/zoom and pan;
- pen may operate visible UI controls;
- Pages organizer pen selection/drag remains allowed;
- Draw with Finger setting should exist later and default Off.

Full Page bottom navigation arrows may become palm targets during handwriting. Do not change them prematurely, but when ink is implemented test auto-hide, palm-safe activation, optional hiding, or other mitigation.

MILESTONE 3.1.2 — SPLIT LAYOUT FIX
------------------------------------
The user reported that in the prior Files interface the explanatory/field content for Split overlapped the Split action buttons. The cause was the split block layout: `.compact-field { height: 100%; }` could consume the block height while the button followed outside that sizing assumption.

3.1.2 changes only the Split CSS layout:
- each `.split-tool-block` is an explicit vertical flex container;
- its field has no forced 100% height and no trailing margin;
- the action button occupies its own flex item and is pushed below the field;
- the existing one-column narrow-screen breakpoint remains;
- Split parsing, filenames, ZIP creation, export, Combine, viewer, touch, pen, Presentation, pane state, and JBIG2 behavior are unchanged.

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.1.0

THIS SECTION IS AUTHORITATIVE OVER OLDER STATUS TEXT BELOW.

MILESTONE 3.0.0 VALIDATION
--------------------------
The first export engine was tested successfully by the user on the five recommended curated PDFs plus the real BaakeScan.pdf JBIG2-heavy scan. The user opened, edited, exported, then checked the exported PDFs in Adobe; results appeared correct. Treat structural export of current page order, deletion/duplication, rotations, mixed sizes, scans, and BaakeScan as validated enough to build later manipulation tools on top of it.

MILESTONE 3.1.0 — FILES / DOCUMENT OPERATIONS
----------------------------------------------
The top workspace selector is now View | Pages | Files. Internally the code still uses the existing export workspace identifier to minimize regression risk, but the user-facing Files workspace is the beginning of the future document/library area.

Files currently provides:
- Export current organized document (existing validated engine).
- Extract selected Pages directly to a new PDF, in current Pages order.
- Split every n pages.
- Split by explicit page groups, one output PDF per line; a line may contain comma-separated pages/ranges such as 1-4,8,11-13.
- If Split creates multiple PDFs, package all outputs into one ZIP for one save/share operation. This is the approved iPad strategy with the existing iPad save/preview/share flow.
- Combine two or more open documents into a NEW working document. Users choose which open documents to include and order them with up/down controls. Combine uses each source document's current edited working page arrangement, clones the page instances, shares underlying source data, and leaves originals unchanged. Combining does not automatically export; the combined working document can be further edited then exported.

SMALL WORKFLOW CHANGES IN 3.1.0
--------------------------------
- In Pages, tapping/clicking empty grid space clears all page selection.
- Opening/importing external files automatically returns to View.
- Open still means open a separate document; it never implicitly merges.
- Exporting while in Files naturally leaves the user in Files.

SPLIT / ZIP IMPLEMENTATION NOTE
-------------------------------
JSZip 3.10.1 is loaded from jsDelivr as an ES module and is included in the service-worker external-cache request alongside PDF.js/pdf-lib. Multiple split PDFs are stored in the ZIP without extra compression (STORE) because PDF contents are generally already compressed and extra compression wastes iPad memory/time.

GRAPH-PAPER REFERENCE
---------------------
The distribution now includes `graph-paper-reference.png`, copied from the user's current blank writing slide. Use it as the visual reference for future generated graph-paper pages: white background, very light cyan/blue thin square grid, roughly quarter-inch-style spacing, small clean margin, unobtrusive enough to write over. The generated PDF grid need not be pixel-identical.

REGRESSION-SENSITIVE VIEWER/INPUT STATUS
----------------------------------------
Confirmed before Milestone 3 work on iPad, Surface, and Chromebook:
- finger pan/scroll and two-finger pinch work;
- pen/Pencil does not pan or zoom PDF content;
- pen operates visible buttons and Pages organizer;
- hidden Presentation controls ignore pen hover/tap;
- high-zoom horizontal pan reaches both edges;
- split/single state and JBIG2/WASM rendering are stable in testing.
Do not alter these paths casually during document-engine work.

NEXT MILESTONE 3 ITEMS
----------------------
1. Insert duplicate current page with/without annotations (annotation distinction becomes meaningful once ink exists; until then copies are equivalent).
2. Insert blank page matching current page dimensions/orientation.
3. Insert graph-paper page matching current page dimensions/orientation.
4. Create new one-page blank or graph-paper documents.
5. Page-size normalization/orientation, fit/center/crop, margins.
6. Image-to-PDF assembly improvements.
7. Compression/target maximum size without silently rasterizing selectable text.

----------------------------

Distinguish active/open documents, internally stored projects, and external original/output files.

Full Page bottom navigation arrows may need auto-hide/relocation/palm-safe handling when ink is implemented.

PDF WORKBENCH PWA — MILESTONE 3 CURRENT UPDATE
===============================================

CURRENT BUILD: Milestone 3.0.0
Visible About version: 3.0.0
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m3.0.0-v1

THIS SECTION IS AUTHORITATIVE OVER OLDER STATUS TEXT BELOW.

MILESTONE 2 VALIDATION COMPLETE
-------------------------------
Testing on iPad, Surface, and Chromebook confirmed the Milestone 2 input/viewer foundation is working: finger pan and pinch work; pen/Pencil does not pan or zoom PDF content; pen operates visible controls and the Pages organizer; hidden Presentation controls ignore pen hover/tap; high-zoom horizontal pan reaches both page edges. The compact 28 px footer in 2.1.6 is acceptable on iPad. Treat these behaviors as regression-sensitive.

MILESTONE 3.0.0 — FIRST EXPORT ENGINE
--------------------------------------
A third top-level workspace, Export, is now present alongside View and Pages. Export creates a new PDF from the current working document. The first implementation preserves current page order, deletions, duplications, and user-applied quarter-turn rotations. PDF source pages are structurally copied into the output rather than rasterized. Imported image documents can also be exported; JPEG/PNG are embedded directly, while other browser-decodable image formats may be converted to PNG for the image page only.

Export uses pdf-lib 1.17.1 loaded as an ES module from jsDelivr. The service worker is asked to cache that external module along with the PDF.js main/worker resources so it can be reused after an online load. PDF.js WASM/JBIG2/CMap/standard-font configuration must remain intact.

MILESTONE 3 ROADMAP
-------------------
Recommended sequence after validating 3.0.0 export:
1. Explicit combine/merge; Open must continue to mean open a separate document.
2. Extract selected pages and Split output.
3. Insert duplicate, blank, and graph-paper pages; new blank/graph-paper documents.
4. Page-size normalization/orientation, fit/center/crop, margins.
5. Image-to-PDF assembly.
6. Compression/target maximum size without silently rasterizing selectable text.

SPLIT OUTPUT TARGET
-------------------
Default Split behavior should create external output files, not automatically load every generated part into PDF Workbench. For many outputs, ZIP is appropriate. A later optional “Open resulting files in PDF Workbench” choice may be added, but should not be the default.

DOCUMENT LIBRARY / PERSISTENCE ROADMAP
--------------------------------------
After Milestone 3 manipulation/export, replace the minimal “Recent Projects” idea with a real lightweight Library/Home layer. This should use internal browser storage (IndexedDB/OPFS) because iPad cannot be assumed to retain arbitrary external filesystem handles. Distinguish:
- currently open documents (active session/workspace);
- internally stored documents/projects (persistent working copies);
- external original/output files.

Planned Library behavior:
- Close removes a document from the active workspace without deleting a stored copy/project.
- Reopen from Library/Recent.
- Rename documents.
- Create/rename/delete folders and move documents among folders.
- Search/sort and Recent; favorites may come later.
- Remove from Recent is separate from Delete Stored Copy.
- Clear Local Projects/Storage remains available.
- Library is GoodNotes-like organization, not a full replacement filesystem.
- Project storage eventually preserves page edits, annotations, view positions, split-pane state, zoom/modes, and export settings.

FUTURE INKING NOTE
------------------
Full Page bottom navigation arrows may be vulnerable to palm activation once handwriting is implemented. Test before deciding on auto-hide, edge relocation, palm-safe activation, or an ink-mode hide option.

DISTRIBUTION RULE
-----------------
Every ZIP must contain this comprehensive PDF_Workbench_Project_Handoff.txt and update the current-status section, visible About version, and service-worker/cache version together.

PDF WORKBENCH PWA — CURRENT PROJECT HANDOFF
============================================

IMPORTANT: READ THIS CURRENT-STATUS SECTION FIRST.
The historical handoff follows after it for full architectural detail. Where a historical status statement conflicts with this section, this section is authoritative.

CURRENT BUILD
-------------
Milestone 2.1.6

Visible About version: 2.1.6
Service-worker/cache version: pdf-workbench-m2.1.6-v1

Every distributed ZIP must include this handoff file and update it for the release.

CURRENT PURPOSE / ARCHITECTURE
------------------------------
PDF Workbench is a cross-platform, local-processing PWA for Windows, Surface, iPad/Apple Pencil, and Chromebook. Preserve these architectural rules:

- PDF.js viewing, including WASM/JBIG2/CMap/standard-font resource configuration.
- Opening another PDF opens a separate document; it must never implicitly merge it.
- Document data is shared; viewer-instance state is not.
- Single, left, and right views each own independent document/page/zoom/fit/scroll/view-mode state.
- The same PDF may be open in both split panes at different pages and zooms.
- iPad Presentation is app-level chrome hiding, not Safari native fullscreen.
- Page organizer selection/reordering/rotation/duplication/deletion behavior must not regress.
- Manual PWA Reload Latest Version remains available.

CONFIRMED DEVICE RESULTS THROUGH 2.1.4
--------------------------------------
Testing in Safari on iPad and Chrome on Surface confirmed:

- one-finger pan/scroll works;
- two-finger pinch zoom works;
- high-zoom pan can reach both far-left and far-right page edges;
- pen/Pencil does not pan or zoom PDF content;
- pen operates visible buttons;
- pen hover/tap does not reveal or activate hidden Presentation controls.

Preserve the 2.1.4 explicit input model:

PDF VIEWER SURFACES
- `touch-action: none` prevents browser-native direct manipulation on PDF content.
- Pen/Pencil on PDF content is reserved for annotation and is currently inert.
- One finger pans/scrolls.
- Two fingers pinch zoom and pan with the measured anchor-preserving zoom implementation.
- Full Page uses a vertical one-finger swipe to change pages.
- Visible controls remain operable by pen.

PAGES / ORGANIZER
- Pen may select pages, operate checkboxes, and drag/reorder thumbnails.

PRESENTATION
- Mouse hover near the top may reveal controls.
- Finger tap near the top while hidden reveals controls and consumes the gesture.
- Pen hover/tap on hidden PDF content must not reveal or activate controls.
- Pen may operate controls after they are visible.

MILESTONE 2.1.5 CHANGE
----------------------
On iPad, footer/status text touched the top of the footer while it was vertically centered on Surface. The footer row was fixed at 28 px even though iPad safe-area bottom padding was placed inside that same fixed-height box.

2.1.5 changes the app-shell footer grid row to:

    calc(var(--status-h) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))

while keeping the safe-area padding on the status bar. This preserves the nominal 28 px footer content region and adds separate room for the Home-indicator inset. No gesture or viewer-state code is changed in this release.

PWA UPDATE OBSERVATION
----------------------
During 2.1.4 testing, the installed iPad PWA temporarily failed to pick up the latest build through Reload latest version even though Safari could load it. The PWA later updated successfully. Treat this as transient unless it becomes reproducible. Do not overhaul the update mechanism solely because of this one occurrence.

FULL PAGE NAVIGATION / FUTURE INKING
------------------------------------
The Full Page mode currently places navigation arrows at the bottom of the screen. Once inking is added, these may be vulnerable to accidental palm activation while writing. Do not change them prematurely, but explicitly test this during ink/palm-rejection work. Possible later responses include auto-hide, palm-safe activation, moving them toward edges, or allowing them to be hidden in ink mode.

ANNOTATION INPUT POLICY FOR LATER
---------------------------------
- Draw with Finger: OFF by default.
- Finger navigation remains active while Pen/Eraser/Select is selected.
- Pen + Pen tool: draw.
- Pen + Eraser: erase.
- Pen + Select/Lasso: select annotations.
- Do not use pen hover to reveal Presentation controls.
- A future explicit Hand/Pan tool may optionally permit stylus panning.

NEW PAGE / NOTES FEATURES ALREADY REQUESTED
-------------------------------------------
After the current page, support insertion of:
- copy of current page with annotations;
- copy of current page without annotations;
- blank page same size/orientation as current page;
- graph-paper page same size/orientation as current page, with light marks and approximately 1/4-inch squares.

Consider exposing the same insertion commands in Presentation, normal View, and Pages/Document view.

Allow creating a new document that begins as one blank page or one graph-paper page. This lets PDF Workbench serve as a notes application once inking exists.

NEXT DEVELOPMENT DIRECTION
--------------------------
Treat the Milestone 2 viewer/input foundation as substantially stable after 2.1.5 unless regression testing reveals a new issue.

Next planned major phase: Milestone 3 document handling/export.

Recommended order:
1. Export the current edited document, preserving page order, rotations, duplicates, and deletions.
2. Explicit combine/merge; opening documents must remain separate unless Combine is invoked.
3. Extract selected pages and split output (every n pages, ranges, one page per output where useful).
4. Add/insert duplicate, blank, and graph-paper pages; allow new blank/graph-paper documents.
5. Normalize page size/orientation, fit/center/crop, and margins.
6. Image-to-PDF.
7. Target-size compression without silently rasterizing selectable text.

Likely top-level interface remains Pages | View | Export. Pages handles structural editing; View handles reading/presentation/annotation; Export is a lightweight output/settings surface. Do not add a permanent Documents workspace unless actual use shows it is needed.

IMMEDIATE 2.1.5 TESTS
---------------------
1. iPad PWA: footer text should be vertically centered above the Home-indicator safe area.
2. Surface/desktop: footer should remain vertically centered.
3. Reconfirm one-finger pan and two-finger pinch.
4. Reconfirm pen does not pan/zoom PDF content but operates visible buttons.
5. Reconfirm hidden Presentation controls ignore pen hover/tap.
6. Reconfirm Pages organizer pen drag still works.
7. About must report Milestone 2.1.6.
8. Reload Latest Version should remain available; investigate further only if failure becomes reproducible.

PDF WORKBENCH PWA — COMPLETE PROJECT HANDOFF
============================================

Purpose of this file
--------------------
This file is intended to be uploaded at the start of a new ChatGPT conversation so development can continue without reconstructing the long prior chat.

The project is a cross-platform PDF workbench / viewer / annotator being built incrementally as a hosted Progressive Web App (PWA). It is intended for:

- Windows desktop/laptop
- Windows Surface-style touch/stylus devices
- iPad with Apple Pencil
- Chromebook, including touch/stylus models where available

The application should feel much lighter than Acrobat and use less permanent screen space than Goodnotes, especially in classroom projection. It should support strong PDF manipulation as well as a future annotation system.

The current tested build at the end of the prior conversation is:

    Milestone 2.1.0

The About dialog must ALWAYS show the current build/version number, and every new build must also bump the service-worker/cache version so the user can verify which cached/deployed build is actually running.

A manual PWA “reload latest version” / update function has been added and has been confirmed to work on iPad.


====================================================================
1. OVERALL PRODUCT GOAL
====================================================================

Build a local-processing PDF workbench with three broad working modes:

1. Organize / Pages
2. View / Annotate
3. Export

The app itself may be hosted, but PDF processing should happen locally in the browser. User PDFs do not need to be uploaded to a server.

Important philosophy:

- Touch-first, but excellent on Windows desktop too.
- Support mouse, trackpad, finger, stylus, Apple Pencil.
- Minimal permanent interface chrome.
- Full-screen / presentation-style viewing is central.
- Navigation and annotation tool state must be independent.
- The user should be able to scroll, zoom, change pages, switch files, or switch panes while Pen/Eraser/Select remains active.
- Document data and view-instance state must be separated.
- The same PDF may be displayed in two independent panes at different pages and zoom levels.
- Editing should be non-destructive until export.
- Recent local projects/files are desirable.
- The final app should be useful both for classroom presentation/annotation and general PDF assembly/cleanup.


====================================================================
2. ORIGINALLY REQUESTED PDF CAPABILITIES
====================================================================

The user initially asked for a general PDF tool that can:

- Combine images into a PDF.
- Combine PDFs.
- Mix PDFs and images in one output.
- Fix page size.
- Fix page orientation.
- Rotate pages.
- Compress PDFs to a maximum target file size.
- Rearrange pages.
- Delete pages.
- Duplicate pages.
- Extract selected pages.
- Split a PDF into groups of n pages.
- Potentially split by specified page ranges or one page per output.
- Normalize pages to Letter, Legal, A4, first-page size, or custom dimensions.
- Fit/center/crop content.
- Add margins / expand page canvas.
- Batch-process documents.
- Eventually support scan cleanup such as:
  - auto-crop
  - deskew
  - grayscale
  - black-and-white
  - blank-page detection
  - contrast/background cleanup
- Eventually support page numbering / simple headers/footers.
- ZIP multiple output PDFs where helpful.

OCR, editing existing PDF text, redaction, forms, digital signatures, bookmarks, etc. are NOT current priorities.


====================================================================
3. PWA ARCHITECTURE
====================================================================

The app is being built as a PWA because the user wants one application that works on Windows, iPad, Surface, and Chromebook.

Benefits/requirements:

- Can be installed to Home Screen / desktop.
- Can run in a cleaner standalone window.
- Can cache its own app shell for offline use.
- Can maintain persistent settings/projects in browser storage.
- Same codebase across platforms.
- Files themselves remain local.

Important file-access design:

- Chromium on Windows/Chromebook can optionally use File System Access APIs when available.
- iPad/Safari cannot be assumed to support the same arbitrary persistent file handles.
- Therefore cross-platform “Recent Projects” should use internal local copies/project storage in browser storage (IndexedDB/OPFS).
- Do NOT rely on unrestricted desktop-like filesystem access.

PWA update behavior:

- Every release must bump the visible About version.
- Every release must bump the service-worker/cache version.
- The user should be able to force an update via About → Reload latest version / Check for Updates.
- This manual update path has been tested successfully on iPad.
- Do not require deleting/reinstalling the Home Screen PWA for updates.


====================================================================
4. MAJOR TECHNICAL COMPONENTS
====================================================================

Current/expected architecture:

- PDF.js for PDF rendering/viewing.
- pdf-lib or similar for eventual PDF page/output manipulation.
- Custom annotation layer rather than relying on PDF.js annotation editor.
- IndexedDB / OPFS for local project persistence later.
- Service worker for PWA/offline/update behavior.
- Pointer Events for mouse/touch/stylus unification.
- Non-destructive document/page model.
- View-instance state separate from document state.

The downloadable test builds have been provided as ZIP files and run from a local HTTP server for desktop testing.

When testing locally on Windows, the app must be served over HTTP rather than opened with file:// because service workers/PWA behavior require HTTP/HTTPS.


====================================================================
5. DOCUMENT MODEL — IMPORTANT ARCHITECTURAL RULES
====================================================================

Opening another PDF must NOT implicitly merge it into the current document.

This bug existed in Milestone 1 and was corrected.

“Open” means:
    open another separate document.

“Combine/Merge” will later be an explicit operation.

Each document should have its own document-level state:

- source PDF/image data
- pages / page instances
- page order
- rotations
- crops/transforms
- deletions/duplications
- annotations later
- document-level undoable edits

Each viewer pane/view instance should have its own independent viewing state:

- which document it shows
- current page
- scroll position
- zoom percentage
- Fit Page / Fit Width / manual zoom
- Continuous / Page Snap / Full Page mode
- focus/active-pane state

Critical rule:

    THE DOCUMENT IS SHARED; THE VIEW IS NOT.

This matters especially when the SAME PDF is open in both left and right panes.

Example desired behavior:

LEFT:
    BaakeScan.pdf
    page 12
    80%
    Continuous

RIGHT:
    BaakeScan.pdf
    page 47
    120%
    Full Page

Scrolling/zooming/changing pages in one pane must NOT affect the other.

Later, annotations are document-level:
- If both panes show page 20 of the same PDF and the user writes on page 20 in one pane, the annotation should appear in the other pane too.
- But viewport position/zoom remains independent.


====================================================================
6. PAGE ORGANIZER / THUMBNAIL FUNCTIONALITY
====================================================================

The Pages/organizer portion has been tested fairly extensively and is currently considered largely functional across:

- Windows
- iPad
- Surface
- Chromebook

Existing functionality includes:

- thumbnails
- page selection
- multiple selection
- Shift-range selection
- checkbox-based additive selection
- rotate
- duplicate
- delete
- undo/redo
- drag reorder
- multiple open documents

Important selection behavior agreed upon:

Thumbnail checkbox:
- Checking a page adds it to selection.
- Checking another page adds that page too; Ctrl is not required.
- Unchecking removes only that page.
- Shift-clicking a checkbox applies checked/unchecked state across the range from the selection anchor.
- Checkbox visuals must immediately match actual selection.

Thumbnail/page preview selection:
- Normal click can select one page.
- Ctrl-click toggles individual pages on desktop.
- Shift-click selects a range.

Drag/reorder bugs already fixed:
- Early version moved only one position at a time.
- Added drag ghost.
- Added visible placeholder.
- Added long-distance/multi-row dragging.
- Added reflow animation.
- Added edge auto-scroll.
- Fixed premature empty slot appearing immediately after click.
- Drag placeholder now begins only after a drag threshold.
- iPad native text/image selection during thumbnail dragging is suppressed.
- iPad dragging has been tested and works.

Thumbnail fit bug already fixed:
- Thumbnails now use true contain-to-fit behavior so the whole page is visible.
- Consistent preview wells are used for odd page sizes/orientations.


====================================================================
7. LARGE SCANNED PDF / JBIG2 BUG — IMPORTANT FIX
====================================================================

A large scanned PDF called:

    BaakeScan.pdf

was used as a test case.

Initially many pages appeared completely blank:
- in thumbnails
- in the main viewer
- on Windows
- on iPad
- rerendering/scrolling away and back did not fix them

The same pages displayed normally in Adobe Acrobat and Chrome’s built-in viewer.

Inspection of the actual PDF showed:
- 58 PDF pages
- 20 pages used ordinary JPEG images
- 38 pages used JBIG2 compression

The blank-page pattern corresponded to JBIG2 scan pages.

Root cause:
The custom PDF.js integration had configured the PDF.js worker but had NOT configured its WebAssembly decoder/resource paths.

Recent PDF.js versions use a WASM-based JBIG2 decoder, so the custom integration needed the correct resource configuration.

Fix implemented:
Provide PDF.js with:
- wasmUrl
- packed CMaps / cMapUrl as appropriate
- standardFontDataUrl
- WebAssembly support / decoder resources

After this change, the previously blank pages appeared normally.

IMPORTANT:
Do not remove or regress this configuration.
JBIG2 is common in scanned PDFs, so this is a required permanent compatibility feature.

Large scanned PDFs should also continue to use bounded canvas/rendering memory:
- do not eagerly retain every page object/canvas at high resolution
- render near the viewport
- release far-away canvases
- keep thumbnails lower resolution
- avoid excessive simultaneous raster renders


====================================================================
8. VIEWER MODES
====================================================================

Three viewer modes are implemented/planned as primary modes:

1. Continuous
   - Normal vertical scrolling.
   - Can stop with portions of two pages visible.

2. Page Snap
   - Free vertical scrolling.
   - Settles/aligns to a useful page position when scrolling ends.

3. Full Page
   - One full page at a time.
   - Swipe/wheel/page buttons move page-by-page.

Fit/zoom modes:

- Fit Page
- Fit Width
- Manual zoom percentage
- Desktop − / percentage / + controls
- Ctrl+wheel/trackpad zoom
- Pinch-to-zoom on touch devices

Zoom range currently roughly:
    25% to 400%

Fit Page and Fit Width should have DISTINCT icons.

A current design issue:
The Full Page viewing icon has looked too much like the Single-layout icon in Presentation mode.
This should be corrected in the next revision with clearly different iconography.


====================================================================
9. PRESENTATION MODE
====================================================================

Presentation mode is central to classroom use.

Goal:
The document gets the screen; controls appear only when needed.

Windows/desktop:
- True browser fullscreen is acceptable.

iPad:
- Do NOT invoke native Safari fullscreen.
- Native iPad fullscreen uses a downward swipe system gesture that conflicts with vertical PDF scrolling.
- Instead use app-level “chrome hidden” Presentation mode inside the PWA/browser content area.
- This has been tested and works much better, especially when installed from Home Screen.

Presentation toolbar behavior:

- Hidden by default.
- Appears when user moves/taps near the top.
- Auto-hides after a short delay.
- After using a toolbar button, auto-hide timer should restart.
- Hidden controls must NOT receive accidental clicks/taps.
- A tap whose purpose is merely to reveal the hidden toolbar must be consumed and must not also activate a button beneath it.

This tap-through bug previously existed.
As of Milestone 2.1.0:
- Surface testing indicates the hidden-control tap-through fix seems to work in ordinary cases.
- However, one earlier Chromebook/Presentation test found that when scrolled all the way to the top, touching the top could still hit a hidden-menu button.
- Milestone 2.1.0 was intended to fix this. Continue testing on iPad/Chromebook.

Presentation toolbar sizing:

Current requirement:
Do NOT progressively squeeze/shrink labeled buttons.

Use essentially a two-state toolbar:
- Wide enough: icon + text label
- Not wide enough: icon only

Keep practical touch/click target size.
Do not shrink buttons/text/padding through awkward intermediate sizes.

If still too narrow:
- remove labels
- tighten gaps moderately
- move secondary controls into overflow
- only use horizontal scrolling as a last resort

Surface testing of Milestone 2.1.0 found narrow-window button resizing still not acceptable.
The user specifically requested the simpler:
    labeled buttons → icons only
transition.


====================================================================
10. MULTI-DOCUMENT / SPLIT VIEW
====================================================================

Milestone 2.0.0 added split view.

Features already present:

- Two documents side by side.
- Each pane has its own document selector.
- Tap/click pane to make it active.
- Active pane indicated visually.
- Main viewer controls act on active pane.
- Each pane can have independent:
  - document
  - zoom
  - fit mode
  - current page
  - scroll position
  - viewing mode
- Full Page navigation can be controlled independently.
- Touch scrolling and pinch zoom intended to work independently.
- Split view works in Presentation mode.
- In split Presentation mode, L/R controls select which pane toolbar actions affect.

Important desired capability:
The SAME PDF may be open in both panes at different pages/zooms.
This is intentionally useful for teaching and is something Goodnotes cannot easily do without iPad multitasking.

Single/Split behavior required:

Single → Split:
- The current Single document should transfer into the active split pane at the same page/zoom/scroll state.
- The other split pane should restore its previous state if available.
- If no previous other-pane document exists, it may choose a sensible most-recently-used other document.

Split → Single:
- Whichever pane is active should become the Single view.
- Preserve its exact visible state.
- Preserve the inactive pane state in memory.

Single → Split again:
- The pane that had become Single should receive the updated Single state.
- The other pane should reappear exactly where it was left.

Example:

Start split:
LEFT = PDF A page 12 at 80%
RIGHT = PDF A page 47 at 120%

Make LEFT active → switch to Single.
In Single move to page 18, zoom 90%.
Switch back to Split.

Expected:
LEFT = PDF A page 18 at 90%
RIGHT = PDF A page 47 at 120%

Current status:
Milestone 2.1.0 improved Single↔Split transfer considerably on Surface.
Surface test said:
- selecting active pane from split and transferring seems to work better

However, before 2.1.0 there was a specific aliasing bug:
- If right pane was active
- choose a document
- switch to Single
- switch back to Split
- both panes could become the original right-pane document

2.1.0 was specifically intended to fix this by making left/right/single separate view instances rather than aliased state.

Continue testing this on:
- iPad
- Chromebook
- Surface


====================================================================
11. PINCH ZOOM — CURRENT UNRESOLVED BUG
====================================================================

This is the main known viewer bug after Milestone 2.1.0.

Observed on:
- Surface
- Chromebook
- iPad

Behavior:
When pinch-zooming, the visible scroll/document position jumps.

Desired behavior:
The PDF point under the midpoint of the user’s fingers before zoom should remain under the midpoint after zoom.

Correct algorithm concept:

1. Determine the pinch midpoint in viewer coordinates.
2. Determine the exact PDF/document coordinate under that midpoint BEFORE scaling.
3. Apply new scale.
4. Wait until the page/layout dimensions reflect the new zoom.
5. Measure actual post-layout geometry.
6. Adjust scrollLeft/scrollTop so the same PDF/document coordinate returns to the same screen midpoint.

Important:
The previous 2.1.0 approach tried to anchor pinch zoom but Surface still jumps.
Do NOT keep merely tuning the same ratio-only math.
The next revision should use actual before/after layout measurement.

Each pane must perform this independently.
The same document shown in both panes must not have one pane’s pinch zoom affect the other.


====================================================================
12. ZOOM / VIEW STATE BY DOCUMENT AND BY PANE
====================================================================

Earlier builds added per-document zoom preservation in single-document viewing.
This worked on iPad Safari.

However, split view revealed the need for a more precise distinction:

Document-level preferences may include:
- perhaps preferred fit/zoom defaults

But actual active view state must belong to VIEW INSTANCES, not just documents.

Therefore:

Single view state:
- document
- page
- scroll
- zoom
- fit mode
- viewer mode

Left pane view state:
- document
- page
- scroll
- zoom
- fit mode
- viewer mode

Right pane view state:
- document
- page
- scroll
- zoom
- fit mode
- viewer mode

This prevents one pane from affecting another when the same document is shown twice.

Document edits remain shared.


====================================================================
13. RELOAD/PERSISTENCE BEHAVIOR
====================================================================

Some versions seemed to remember split/single or viewer state inconsistently across reloads.

Agreed behavior until Recent Projects exists:

Only intentional app/UI preferences should persist across reloads.

Transient pane/document state should NOT partially survive in confusing ways.

Do not let accidental browser/session state cause:
- random split/single mode on reload
- stale active pane
- pane document aliasing
- half-restored scroll/zoom

Later, when Recent Projects is implemented, project/view state persistence can be explicit and reliable.


====================================================================
14. ANNOTATION SYSTEM — FUTURE REQUIREMENTS
====================================================================

The user currently uses Goodnotes on iPad for comparison.

Goodnotes works well, but its menus consume too much classroom screen space.
The new app should retain a Goodnotes-quality pen experience while using much less permanent chrome.

The user currently does NOT need shapes/text/arrows as primary annotation tools.

Initial annotation toolbar should focus on:

- Pen
- Eraser
- Select/Lasso
- Undo/Redo

Navigation must remain available without leaving annotation mode.

Important behavior:

If Pen is active:
- stylus/Pencil writes
- one finger scrolls/pans
- two fingers pinch zoom
- page buttons change pages
- document switching works
- pane switching works
- scroll mode switching works
- Pen stays active throughout

Likewise for Eraser/Select.


====================================================================
15. INKING REQUIREMENTS
====================================================================

Ink should NOT be raw straight line segments between pointer samples.

Use smooth local cubic interpolation, likely:
- Catmull–Rom → cubic Bézier
or a similar local spline method.

Do NOT use a highly global spline that creates oscillations.

For mathematical handwriting:
- smoothing must be conservative
- preserve sharp deliberate corners
- do not “correct” symbols excessively

Potential pipeline:

raw pointer samples
    ↓
remove near-duplicates
    ↓
light positional filtering
    ↓
local cubic interpolation
    ↓
pressure smoothing
    ↓
render variable-width stroke

Store original/sample data so strokes remain editable.

Stylus data may include:
- x
- y
- pressure
- timestamp
- potentially tilt/angle

Use coalesced pointer events where available for higher-frequency input.
Use predicted samples where available for lower visual latency.

Predicted ink is temporary only and must be replaced by real samples.


====================================================================
16. PALM REJECTION
====================================================================

Required.

Default desired behavior:

Stylus/Pencil:
    draws

Finger/palm while stylus is actively drawing:
    ignored as drawing input

Finger when stylus is not drawing:
    scroll/pan

Two fingers:
    pinch/zoom and pan

Optional setting:
    Draw with Finger = Off by default

Use Pointer Events to distinguish:
- pen
- touch
- mouse

This needs actual testing on:
- Apple Pencil
- Surface pen
- Chromebook stylus if available


====================================================================
17. ANNOTATION OBJECT MODEL
====================================================================

Annotations must remain editable vector-like objects in project state rather than being burned immediately into pixels/PDF.

A pen stroke should conceptually store:

- id
- pageId
- original sample points
- smoothed path
- color
- opacity
- width
- pressure information
- transforms

Because strokes are objects, later operations should support:

- tap select
- lasso selection
- rectangle selection
- select several strokes
- move
- cut
- copy
- paste
- duplicate
- delete
- change color
- change width
- change opacity
- eventually resize
- eventually group/ungroup if useful

Internal annotation clipboard should be app-local rather than relying on system clipboard permissions.

Example desired workflow:
- Lasso handwriting.
- Copy.
- Change page.
- Paste.
- Move.
- Recolor.

The clipboard may work across open documents in the app.


====================================================================
18. ERASER
====================================================================

Start with stroke eraser:

Touch any part of a stroke:
    delete entire stroke

Later add optional partial/path eraser:
- geometrically cut a stroke
- remaining portions become separate stroke objects


====================================================================
19. ANNOTATION EXPORT
====================================================================

Two kinds of save/output are desired:

Save Project:
- annotations remain editable
- page manipulations remain editable/non-destructive

Export PDF:
- annotations are written into the output PDF

Where practical:
- export handwriting as vector paths rather than screenshots
- pressure-sensitive strokes may be exported as filled vector outlines

Explicit:
    Flatten annotations

may be offered.


====================================================================
20. RECENT FILES / PROJECTS
====================================================================

Desired later.

Start screen could show:

Open Files
Recent Projects

A project should eventually preserve:

- source PDFs/images or internal copies
- page order
- rotations
- crops
- deleted/duplicated pages
- annotations
- viewer positions
- split-pane states
- zooms
- scroll modes
- export settings

On iPad, because persistent arbitrary filesystem handles cannot be assumed, keep internal local working copies.

Provide explicit controls:
- Remove from Recent
- Delete Stored Copy
- Clear Local Projects/Storage


====================================================================
21. EXPORT / PDF MANIPULATION ENGINE — NEXT MAJOR FUNCTIONAL AREA
====================================================================

After Milestone 2 viewer state is stable, the planned next major phase is the actual output/manipulation engine.

Priority order:

1. Export current rearranged/rotated/duplicated page order.
2. Explicit merge/combine.
3. Extract selected pages.
4. Split every n pages.
5. Split by selected ranges/page numbers.
6. Image-to-PDF.
7. Normalize page size/orientation.
8. Crop/fit/center/margins.
9. Compression to target maximum size.
10. Batch operations / ZIP outputs.

Compression design:

First preserve structure where possible.

Progressive strategy:
1. Least destructive pass.
2. Recompress raster images.
3. Adjust image resolution/JPEG quality.
4. Iterate toward target file size.
5. If a strict maximum cannot otherwise be met, offer explicit rasterization fallback.

Important:
Do NOT silently rasterize selectable text just to hit a target.
If rasterization is required to guarantee file size, make it an explicit option.


====================================================================
22. UNDO/REDO
====================================================================

One command-history architecture should eventually cover:

- draw stroke
- move stroke
- recolor stroke
- delete annotation
- move page
- delete page
- rotate page
- duplicate page

Do NOT store full PDF snapshots for every undo step.


====================================================================
23. PERFORMANCE RULES
====================================================================

Especially important for iPad and large scanned PDFs:

- Render only visible/near-visible full pages.
- Generate thumbnails separately at low resolution.
- Do not keep every page as a high-res canvas.
- Release far-away canvases.
- Keep original PDF bytes only once.
- Store page operations as references rather than copied PDF pages.
- Store ink as vectors/sample data, not bitmap layers.
- Use workers where practical.
- Show genuine progress for long exports/compression.
- Bound concurrent raster rendering.
- Preserve the JBIG2/WASM configuration.

Windows desktop:

- large scanned JBIG2 PDF works after decoder fix

   - left page 10 at 70%

====================================================================
29. UI/UX PRINCIPLES TO PRESERVE
====================================================================

- Document gets the screen.
- Controls should be easy to find but take little permanent space.
- Presentation mode should be especially clean for classroom display.
- No hover-only functionality.
- Touch targets should stay comfortably tappable.
- Do not shrink buttons progressively into unusability.
- Prefer contextual controls.
- Pen/eraser/select state should survive navigation.
- Switching files should be fast.
- Split view should be a major feature, not an afterthought.
- Same document in both panes at different locations is explicitly desired.
- Full Page and Single layout icons must be visually distinguishable.
- Fit Page and Fit Width must be visually distinguishable.
- Avoid accidental destructive editing in Presentation mode.


====================================================================
30. WHAT TO DO IN A NEW CHAT
====================================================================

When this file is uploaded in a new chat, continue from here rather than redesigning the project.

The first task should be:

1. Read this entire handoff.
2. Treat Milestone 2.1.0 as the current baseline.
3. Ask only if a genuinely necessary file/build is missing.
4. Implement the next consolidated viewer revision addressing:
   - pinch anchor jump
   - labeled→icon-only toolbar breakpoint
   - distinct Full Page icon
   - any remaining Single↔Split state bugs
   - hidden presentation tap-through if still reproducible
5. Keep About/version and service-worker/cache version synchronized.
6. Provide a downloadable ZIP for testing.
7. Continue cross-platform testing on Windows, Surface, iPad, and Chromebook.

Do not remove previously working fixes, especially:
- separate-document architecture
- independent pane/view state
- thumbnail drag improvements
- selection behavior
- iPad non-native Presentation mode
- PDF.js JBIG2/WASM resource configuration
- manual PWA update function
- version display in About

END OF HANDOFF

Milestone 5.0.7 test/fix note (2026-09-01)
- iPad Apple Pencil: 5.0.5 stopped the errant native Copy / Look Up selections, but testing still showed intermittent whole short strokes being missed. A user export (Graph Paper-edited (1).pdf) visibly demonstrates dropped Pencil contacts. 5.0.7 makes pen-start capture more defensive: it no longer rejects Pencil pointerdown based solely on the browser's `button` value, resolves a page stage by hit-testing when WebKit retargets the event, can recover a down-missed contact from a pressure/buttons-bearing pointermove, and preserves an otherwise up-only very short contact as a dot. This is intentionally not a smoothing/interpolation change.
- Local Library rename/export naming: export/compress/extract/split suggestion cache keys now include the current document name, not only document ID. Renaming an already-selected/open Library document should therefore immediately affect the next default export name.
- More (...) menu: raised above the Milestone 5 annotation toolbar so it no longer renders behind the tool strip.
- Still pending after current testing: restore prior open session/workspace after restart; add clean-template creation option; discuss bottom-of-document upward-swipe page creation and what page/template/background it should append; later stroke smoothing/interpolation; partial-stroke eraser and lasso/select.

Milestone 5.0.8 test note (2026-09-01)
- Re-test rapid two-contact writing on iPad (rows of +, i, =, and Q tails are especially useful). If strokes still drop, download diagnostics again; 5.0.8 now reveals whether the missing contact exists in Safari's stylus TouchEvent stream even when absent from Pointer Events.
- Verify More > menu is visually above the annotation toolbar in ordinary View and Presentation.


Milestone 5.0.9 test note (2026-09-01)
- Surface: verify Pen hover no longer shows the plus/crosshair cursor; verify writing remains stable.
- Chromebook: in Pen mode, write naturally with palm resting on the display. The page should remain stable. Then move the pen away and verify deliberate one-finger pan and two-finger pinch still work after the short touch-intent delay. Download diagnostics if a palm still moves the page; look for `palm-touch-suppressed` and `touch-navigation-intentional`.
- iPad: confirm the 5.0.8 Apple Pencil reliability and native-selection suppression remain unchanged.

- Selection action icons now replace the text labels for Delete / Duplicate / Copy / Region / Paste, with full tooltip and aria labels preserved.
- Presentation-mode temporary Files round trips (Assets / Template Manager) no longer exit and re-enter Presentation, which removes the visible blip when going to Files and back.


## Milestone 5.7.21 — editable 90° selection rotation

- Select now includes a compact clockwise-rotate action. One tap rotates the entire current selection 90° clockwise around the selection center.
- Ink and Highlighter remain editable vector/stroke annotations; their stored points are transformed rather than rasterized.
- Image annotations remain editable image objects. A quarter-turn rotation field preserves orientation through movement, proportional resize, copy/paste, Assets/snippets, Local Library persistence, Undo/Redo, and PDF export.
- Mixed selections rotate as one group. No Pen, touch, pinch, or viewer-navigation machinery was changed.


## Milestone 5.7.21 — Local Library multi-file export
- Local Library document cards now have export-selection checkboxes.
- Multiple Library files can be exported directly without opening them first; one selected file exports as PDF, multiple selected files export as a ZIP of PDFs.
- Existing folder PDF export behavior is unchanged.
